# H 開頭詞條

> 本檔收錄 3,913 條含中文對譯的詞目。

<a id="entry-h-000001"></a>
## h

*n.* 1. **氫, 氫氣** — a nonmetallic univalent element that is normally a colorless and odorless highly flammable diatomic gas; the simplest and lightest and most abundant element in the universe
2. **亨利** — a unit of inductance in which an induced electromotive force of one volt is produced when the current is varied at the rate of one ampere per second
3. **普朗克常數** — the constant of proportionality relating the energy of a photon to its frequency; approximately 6.626 x 10^-34 joule-second
4. the 8th letter of the Roman alphabet
5. (thermodynamics) a thermodynamic quantity equal to the internal energy of a system plus the product of its volume and pressure
*“enthalpy is the amount of energy in a system capable of doing mechanical work”*

<a id="entry-h-000002"></a>
## h-bomb

*n.* 1. **氫彈** — a nuclear weapon that releases atomic energy by union of light (hydrogen) nuclei at high temperatures to form helium

<a id="entry-h-000003"></a>
## H-hour

*n.* 1. *(figuratively)* **H時 /H時** — The hour at which any major event planned for the future is set to begin, as used in United States military.

<a id="entry-h-000004"></a>
## h-shaped

*adj.* 1. **H形狀+的** — shaped in the form of the letter H

<a id="entry-h-000005"></a>
## h.p.

*n.* 1. **馬力** — a unit of power equal to 746 watts

<a id="entry-h-000006"></a>
## h2o

*n.* 1. **水** — binary compound that occurs at room temperature as a clear colorless odorless tasteless liquid; freezes into ice below 0 degrees centigrade and boils above 100 degrees centigrade; widely used as a solvent

## habilimented

*adj.* 1. **打扮+的, 裝扮+的** — dressed or clothed especially in fine attire; often used in combination

<a id="entry-h-000020"></a>
## habit

**發音：** /ˈhæb.ɪt/
*n.* 1. **習慣** — an established custom
*“it was their habit to dine at 7 every evening”*
2. **習慣** — (psychology) an automatic pattern of behavior in reaction to a specific situation; may be inherited or acquired through frequent repetition
*“owls have nocturnal habits”*
*“she had a habit twirling the ends of her hair”*
3. **宗教服裝** — a distinctive attire worn by a member of a religious order
4. *(countable, uncountable)* **習慣 /習慣** — An action performed on a regular basis.
5. *(countable, uncountable)* **習慣 /習慣** — An action performed repeatedly and automatically, usually without awareness.
6. *(countable, uncountable)* **修女服** — A long piece of clothing worn by monks and nuns.
*v.* 1. put a habit on

<a id="entry-h-000021"></a>
## habitable zone

*n.* 1. **宜居帶 /宜居帶, 適居帶 /適居帶** — The zone around a star where a planet could experience temperatures like those on Earth, allowing for the possible existence of liquid water and of life.

<a id="entry-h-000022"></a>
## habitant

*n.* 1. **住戶, 居民** — a person who inhabits a particular place

<a id="entry-h-000023"></a>
## habitat

**發音：** /ˈhæb.ɪ.tæt/
*n.* 1. **棲息地** — the type of environment in which an organism or group normally lives or occurs
*“a marine habitat”*
*“he felt safe on his home grounds”*
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **棲息地 /棲息地** — A place in which a person lives.

<a id="entry-h-000024"></a>
## habitation

*n.* 1. **住所, 住處, 住宅** — housing that someone is living in
*“he built a modest dwelling near the pond”*
*“they raise money to provide homes for the homeless”*
2. the native habitat or home of an animal or plant
3. the act of dwelling in or living permanently in a place (said of both animals and men)
*“he studied the creation and inhabitation and demise of the colony”*

<a id="entry-h-000025"></a>
## habitual

**發音：** /həˈbɪ.tʃʊ.əl/
*adj.* 1. **習慣+的, 平常+的, 慣常+的** — commonly used or practiced; usual
*“his accustomed thoroughness”*
*“took his customary morning walk”*
2. **慣常的 /慣常的** — Of or relating to a habit; established as a habit; performed over and over again; recurrent, recurring.
3. **習慣的 /習慣的** — Of a person or thing: engaging in some behaviour as a habit or regularly.

<a id="entry-h-000026"></a>

2. a general accommodation to unchanging environmental conditions

<a id="entry-h-000032"></a>
## hack

**發音：** /hæk/
*n.* 1. **鋤頭** — a tool (as a hoe or pick or mattock) used for breaking up the surface of the soil
2. **出租汽車, 出租車, 的士, 計程車** — a car driven by a person whose job is to take passengers where they want to go in exchange for money
3. **老馬** — an old or over-worked horse
4. *(derogatory)* **文手, 寫手 /寫手** — An untalented writer.
5. **出租的馬** — a horse kept for hire
6. **作為運輸工具的馬** — a saddle horse used for transportation rather than sport etc.
*v.* 1. *(transitive)* **黑, 黑入, 破解** — To chop or cut down in a rough manner.
2. **做, 對付** — be able to manage or manage successfully
*“I can't hack it anymore”*
*“she could not cut the long days in the office”*
3. **哮咳** — cough spasmodically
*“The patient with emphysema is hacking all day”*
4. cut with a hacking tool
5. cut away
*“he hacked his way through the forest”*
6. kick on the arms

## hacker

**發音：** /hækə/
*n.* 1. **黑客, 駭客 /駭客** — One who uses a computer to gain unauthorized access to data stored in, or to carry out malicious attacks on, computer networks or computer systems.
2. someone who plays golf poorly

4. a programmer for whom computing is its own reward; may enjoy the challenge of breaking into other computers but does no harm

5. one who works hard at boring tasks

<a id="entry-h-000102"></a>
## haemophilia

**發音：** /ˌhiːməˈfɪliə
*n.* 1. *(Commonwealth, countable, uncountable)* **血友病** — Any of several hereditary illnesses that impair the body's ability to control bleeding, usually passed from mother to son.
2. **血友病** — congenital tendency to uncontrolled bleeding; usually affects males and is transmitted from mother to son

<a id="entry-h-000103"></a>

## hair

*n.* 1. **頭髮, 毛髮** — a covering for the body (or parts of it) consisting of a dense growth of threadlike structures (as on the human head); helps to prevent heat loss
*“he combed his hair”*
*“each hair consists of layers of dead keratinized cells”*
2. **絨毛** — filamentous hairlike growth on a plant
*“peach fuzz”*
3. **毛布** — cloth woven from horsehair or camelhair; used for upholstery or stiffening in garments
4. a very small distance or space
*“they escaped by a hair's-breadth”*
*“they lost the election by a whisker”*
5. any of the cylindrical filaments characteristically growing from the epidermis of a mammal
*“there is a hair in my soup”*
6. a filamentous projection or process on an organism

## half

**發音：** /ˈhɑːf/
*n.* 1. **一半, 半** — one of two equal parts of a divisible whole
*“half a loaf”*
*“half an hour”*
2. **半, 一半** — One of two usually roughly equal parts into which anything may be divided, or considered as divided.
3. **半場** — one of two divisions into which some games or performances are divided: the two divisions are separated by an interval
*adj.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **半** — Consisting of a half (½, 50%).
2. consisting of one of two equivalent parts in value or quantity
*“a half chicken”*
*“lasted a half hour”*
3. partial
*“gave me a half smile”*
*“he did only a half job”*
4. (of siblings) related through one parent only
*“a half brother”*
*“half sister”*
*adv.* 1. **大約一半+的** — partially or to the extent of a half
*“he was half hidden by the bushes”*

## hallucination

**發音：** /həˌluːsɪˈneɪʃən/
*n.* 1. **幻覺** — illusory perception; a common symptom of severe mental disorder
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **幻覺 /幻覺, 幻想** — A sensory perception of something that does not exist, often arising from disorder of the nervous system, as in delirium tremens.
3. a mistaken or unfounded opinion or idea
*“he has delusions of competence”*
*“his dreams of vast wealth are a hallucination”*
4. an object perceived during a hallucinatory episode
*“he refused to believe that the angel was a hallucination”*

## halt

*v.* 1. **停止, 停** — come to a halt, stop moving
*“the car stopped”*
*“She stopped in front of a store window”*
2. **使停止, 停止, 終止, 阻塞, 中止** — stop from happening or developing
*“Block his election”*
*“Halt the process”*
3. **止住, 止血** — stop the flow of a liquid
*“staunch the blood flow”*
*“stem the tide”*
4. cause to stop
*“Halt the engines”*
*“Arrest the progress”*
*n.* 1. **停留, 逗留, 滯留** — the state of inactivity following an interruption
*“the negotiations were in arrest”*
*“held them in check”*
2. **停止, 凍結** — an interruption or temporary suspension of progress or movement
*“a halt in the arms race”*
*“a nuclear freeze”*
3. the event of something ending
*“it came to a stop at the bottom of the hill”*
*adj.* 1. disabled in the feet or legs
*“a crippled soldier”*
*“a game leg”*

4. **平衡棒** — either of the rudimentary hind wings of dipterous insects; used for maintaining equilibrium during flight

## hamza

*n.* 1. **哈姆扎, 海姆宰** — A sign used in the written Arabic language representing a glottal stop. Hamza may appear as a stand-alone letter (ء (ʔ)) or most commonly diacritically over or under other letters, e.g. أ (ʔ) (over an alif [ا]), إ (ʔ) (under an alif), ؤ (ʔ) (over a wāw [و]) or ئ (ʔ) (over a dotless yāʾ [ى]). The exact seat of hamza is governed by an orthographic rule – the "seat of hamza rule".

## hap

*n.* 1. an accidental happening
*“he recorded all the little haps and mishaps of his life”*
*v.* 1. **碰巧, 發生** — come to pass
*“What is happening?”*
*“The meeting took place off without an incidence”*

<a id="entry-h-000620"></a>
## hapax legomenon

**發音：** /ˌhæ.pæks ləˈɡɒ.məˌnɒn/
*n.* 1. **孤詞 /孤詞** — A word occurring only once in a given corpus.
2. a word with a special meaning used for a special occasion

<a id="entry-h-000621"></a>
## haphazard

**發音：** /(ˌ)hæpˈhæz.əd/
*adj.* 1. **雜亂無章 /雜亂無章** — Random; chaotic; incomplete; not thorough, constant, or consistent.
2. dependent upon or characterized by chance
*“a haphazard plan of action”*
*“his judgment is rather hit-or-miss”*
3. marked by great carelessness
*“a most haphazard system of record keeping”*
*“slapdash work”*
*adv.* 1. **亂七八糟+地, 草率+地, 隨意+地** — without care; in a slapdash manner
*“the Prime Minister was wearing a grey suit and a white shirt with a soft collar, but his neck had become thinner and the collar stood away from it as if it had been bought haphazard”*

<a id="entry-h-000622"></a>
## haphazardly

*adv.* 1. **亂七八糟+地, 草率+地, 隨意+地** — without care; in a slapdash manner
*“the Prime Minister was wearing a grey suit and a white shirt with a soft collar, but his neck had become thinner and the collar stood away from it as if it had been bought haphazard”*
2. in a random manner
*“the houses were randomly scattered”*
*“bullets were fired into the crowd at random”*

<a id="entry-h-000623"></a>
## haphazardness

*n.* 1. **無序性, 隨機性** — the quality of lacking any predictable order or plan

2. of a cell or organism having a single set of chromosomes

<a id="entry-h-000629"></a>
## haplotype

**發音：** /ˈhap.lə(ʊ).taɪp/
*n.* 1. **單倍型 /單倍型, 單體型 /單體型** — A haploid genotype: a group of alleles that are transmitted together.
2. (genetics) a combination of alleles (for different genes) that are located closely together on the same chromosome and that tend to be inherited together

<a id="entry-h-000630"></a>
## haply

*adv.* 1. **偶爾+地, 偶然+地, 意外+地, 碰巧** — by accident
*“betrayed by a word haply overheard”*

<a id="entry-h-000631"></a>
## happen

**發音：** /ˈhæpən/
*v.* 1. **碰巧, 發生** — come to pass
*“What is happening?”*
*“The meeting took place off without an incidence”*
2. **偶然發生或出現, 碰巧發生或出現** — happen, occur, or be the case in the course of events or by chance
*“It happens that today is my birthday”*
*“These things befell" (Santayana)”*
3. **偶然遇到, 碰巧** — chance to be or do something, without intention or causation
*“I happen to have just what you need!”*
4. **實現, 成為現實** — come into being; become reality
*“Her dream really materialized”*
5. *(intransitive)* **發生 /發生** — To occur or take place.
6. **巧遇, 碰巧, 偶然發現** — come upon, as if by accident; meet with
*“We find this idea in Plato”*
*“I happened upon the most wonderful bakery not very far from here”*

<a id="entry-h-000632"></a>
## happen upon

*v.* 1. **偶然發現, 意外發現, 偶然看見** — find unexpectedly
*“the archeologists chanced upon an old tomb”*
*“she struck a goldmine”*

<a id="entry-h-000633"></a>
## happenstance

*n.* 1. **巧合, 偶然** — an event that might have been arranged although it was really accidental

<a id="entry-h-000634"></a>
## happily

**發音：** /ˈhæp.ɪ.li/
*adv.* 1. **快樂地 /快樂地** — In a happy or cheerful manner; with happiness.
2. **幸運地 /幸運** — By good chance; fortunately, successfully.
3. **欣然地** — With good will; in all happiness; willingly.
4. in a joyous manner
*“they shouted happily”*
5. in an unexpectedly lucky way
*“happily he was not injured”*

<a id="entry-h-000635"></a>
## happily ever after

**發音：** /ˈhæpɪli ˌɛvəɹ‿ˈɑːftə/
*n.* 1. *(idiomatic)* **大團圓 /大團圓** — A story, or a conclusion to a story, in which all the loose ends of the plot are tied up, and all the main characters are left in a state of contentment or happiness.

<a id="entry-h-000636"></a>
## happiness

**發音：** /ˈhæpinɪs/
*n.* 1. **好運氣, 快樂, 幸福, 幸運** — state of well-being characterized by emotions ranging from contentment to intense joy
2. *(uncountable)* **幸福, 快樂 /快樂, 愉快** — The emotion of being happy; joy; elation.
3. emotions experienced when in a state of well-being

<a id="entry-h-000637"></a>
## happy

**發音：** /ˈhæpi/
*adj.* 1. **幸福+的, 快樂+的, 高興+的, 愉快+的** — enjoying or showing or marked by joy or pleasure
*“a happy smile”*
*“spent many happy days on the beach”*
2. **樂意+的, 高興+的** — eagerly disposed to act or to be of service
*“glad to help”*
3. **快樂 /快樂** — Implying “May you have a happy ⁓” or similar; used in phrases to wish someone happiness or good fortune at the time of a festival, celebration, or other event or activity.
4. marked by good fortune
*“a felicitous life”*
*“a happy outcome”*
5. well expressed and to the point
*“a happy turn of phrase”*
*“a few well-chosen words”*

<a id="entry-h-000638"></a>
## happy birthday

**發音：** /ˈhæp.i ˈbɜːθ.deɪ/
*v.* 1. *(colloquial, transitive)* **祝某人生日快樂 /祝某人生日快樂** — To wish (someone) a happy birthday.
*interj.* 1. **生日快樂 /生日快樂, 祝你生日快樂 /祝你生日快樂** — Said or written to a person who is celebrating their birthday in order to convey the good wishes of the speaker or writer (literally, "may you have a happy birthday")

<a id="entry-h-000639"></a>
## happy Easter

*“carefree with his money”*
*“freewheeling urban youths”*

<a id="entry-h-000649"></a>
## haptic

**發音：** /ˈhæptɪk/
*adj.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **觸覺的 /觸覺的** — Of or relating to the sense of touch.
2. **有觸覺+的, 觸覺+的, 觸覺上+的** — of or relating to or proceeding from the sense of touch
*“haptic data”*
*“a tactile reflex”*

## hapticity

<a id="entry-h-000651"></a>

<a id="entry-h-000667"></a>
## hard

**發音：** /hɑːd/
*adj.* 1. **困難+的, 艱難+的, 難, 難以, 艱苦+的** — not easy; requiring great physical or mental effort to accomplish or comprehend or endure
*“a difficult task”*
*“nesting places on the cliffs are difficult of access”*
2. **堅硬+的, 硬+的, 堅固+的** — resisting weight or pressure
3. **摧毀性+的, 兇猛+的** — very strong or vigorous
*“strong winds”*
*“a hard left to the chin”*
4. **艱苦+的, 費勁+的, 非常辛勞+的, 艱難+的, 費力+的** — characterized by effort to the point of exhaustion; especially physical effort
*“worked their arduous way up the mining valley”*
*“a grueling campaign”*
5. **硬** — Resistant to pressure; difficult to break, cut, or penetrate.
6. **無聲+的, 清音+的** — produced without vibration of the vocal cords
*“unvoiced consonants such as `p' and `k' and `s'”*
*adv.* 1. **努力, 認真 /認真** — With much force or effort.
2. with effort or force or vigor
*“the team played hard”*
*“worked hard all day”*
3. with firmness
*“held hard to the railing”*
4. earnestly or intently
*“thought hard about it”*
*“stared hard at the accused”*
5. causing great damage or hardship
*“industries hit hard by the depression”*
*“she was severely affected by the bank's failure”*
6. slowly and with difficulty
*“prejudices die hard”*

<a id="entry-h-000887"></a>
## have

**發音：** /ˈhæv/
*v.* 1. **持有, 佔有, 掌握, 擁有, 有** — have or possess, either in a concrete or an abstract sense
*“She has $1,000 in the bank”*
*“He has got two beautiful daughters”*
2. **有** — have as a feature
*“This restaurant features the most famous chefs in France”*
3. **具有, 佔有, 擁有, 有, 持有** — have ownership or possession of
*“He owns three houses in Florida”*
*“How many cars does she have?”*
4. **有** — have a personal or business relationship with someone
*“have a postdoc”*
*“have an assistant”*
5. **有** — have left
*“I have two years left”*
*“I don't have any money left”*
6. **對抗, 面臨** — be confronted with
*“What do we have here?”*
*“Now we have a fine mess”*
*n.* 1. a person who possesses great material wealth

<a id="entry-h-000967"></a>
## hazard

**發音：** /ˈhæzəd/
*n.* 1. **危險之源, 冒險, 風險, 危險** — a source of danger; a possibility of incurring loss or misfortune
*“drinking alcohol is a health hazard”*
2. **僥倖, 偶然性, 運氣, 機會** — an unknown and unpredictable phenomenon that causes an event to result one way rather than another
*“bad luck caused his downfall”*
*“we ran into each other by pure chance”*
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **危險, 風險 /風險, 危險 /危險** — The chance of suffering harm; danger, peril, risk of loss.
4. **障礙物** — an obstacle on a golf course
*v.* 1. **冒險, 推測** — put forward, of a guess, in spite of possible refutation
*“I am guessing that the price of real estate will rise again”*
*“I cannot pretend to say that you are wrong”*
2. **大膽進行, 敢於, 冒險** — put at risk
*“I will stake my good reputation for this”*
3. **冒險, 賭博, 孤注一擲** — take a risk in the hope of a favorable outcome
*“When you buy these stocks you are gambling”*

<a id="entry-h-000968"></a>

<a id="entry-h-000970"></a>
## hazardous

*adj.* 1. **冒危險+的, 冒險+的, 擔風險+的, 危險+的, 有危險+的** — involving risk or danger
*“skydiving is a hazardous sport”*

## hazardous material

*n.* 1. **危險物質 /危險物質** — A material, natural or man-made, which is intrinsically dangerous or otherwise poses a safety hazard, such as materials which are explosive, poisonous, chemically active (including acids and other corrosives), radioactive, or biologically active (including human blood and other medical waste).

*n.* 1. **危險** — the state of being dangerous

<a id="entry-h-000973"></a>
## haze

**發音：** /heɪz/
*n.* 1. **迷惑** — confusion characterized by lack of clarity
2. *(uncountable, usually)* **霾, 薄霧 /薄霧, 靄 /靄, 煙霧 /煙霧** — Very fine solid particles (smoke, dust) or liquid droplets (moisture) suspended in the air, slightly limiting visibility. (Compare fog, mist.)
3. atmospheric moisture or dust or smoke that causes reduced visibility
*v.* 1. **使變朦朧, 使變糊塗, 懵懂, 迷糊, 迷惑** — become hazy, dull, or cloudy
2. **捉弄, 戲弄** — harass by imposing humiliating or painful tasks, as in military institutions

<a id="entry-h-000974"></a>
## haze over

*v.* 1. **變得有霧 /變得有霧, 變朦朧 /變朦朧, 變得霧濛濛 /變得霧濛濛** — To become covered in haze.
2. **使含糊, 使看不清, 遮掩, 使困惑** — make less visible or unclear
*“The stars are obscured by the clouds”*
*“the big elm tree obscures our view of the valley”*

**發音：** /ˈheɪzəl/

*n.* 1. *(countable, usually)* **榛, 榛樹** — A tree or shrub of the genus Corylus, bearing edible nuts called hazelnuts or filberts.
2. **榛子** — Australian tree grown especially for ornament and its fine-grained wood and bearing edible nuts

4. the fine-grained wood of a hazelnut tree (genus Corylus) and the hazel tree (Australian genus Pomaderris)
5. any of several shrubs or small trees of the genus Corylus bearing edible nuts enclosed in a leafy husk
6. a shade of brown that is yellowish or reddish; it is a greenish shade of brown when used to describe the color of someone's eyes

<a id="entry-h-000993"></a>
## head

**發音：** /hɛd/
*n.* 1. **頭, 腦袋, 頭腦** — the upper part of the human body or the front part of the body in animals; contains the face and brains
*“he stuck his head out the window”*
2. **頭, 頭腦, 腦海, 腦袋** — that which is responsible for one's thoughts and feelings; the seat of the faculty of reason
*“his mind wandered”*
*“I couldn't get his words out of my head”*
3. **領袖, 首腦, 首領** — a person who is in charge
*“the head of the whole operation”*
4. **膿腫頭** — the tip of an abscess (where the pus accumulates)
5. **頭部尺寸** — the length or height based on the size of a human or animal head
*“he is two heads taller than his little sister”*
*“his horse won by a head”*
6. **頭狀花序** — a dense cluster of flowers or foliage
*“a head of cauliflower”*
*“a head of lettuce”*
*v.* 1. **走向, 前進** — to go or travel towards
*“where is she heading”*
*“We were headed for the mountains”*
2. **帶領, 率領, 主導, 引導** — be in charge of
*“Who is heading this project?”*
3. **領路, 帶領** — travel in front of; go in advance of others
*“The procession was headed by John”*
4. **走在前頭, 領頭, 為首** — be the first or leading member of (a group) and excel
*“This student heads the class”*
5. **指揮, 引導, 指導** — direct the course; determine the direction of travelling
6. *(intransitive)* **往** — To move in a specified direction.

3. **中央, 中心, 中心點, 中間** — an area that is approximately central within some larger region
*“it is in the center of town”*
*“they ran forward into the heart of the struggle”*

*“he drew a heart and called it a valentine”*
5. **好感, 慈愛, 愛情** — a positive feeling of liking
*“he had trouble expressing the affection he felt”*
*“the child won everyone's heart”*
6. **紅心** — a playing card in the major suit that has one or more red hearts on it
*“he led the queen of hearts”*
*“hearts were trumps”*

## heat

**發音：** /ˈhiːt/
*v.* 1. **使熱, 加熱** — make hot or hotter
*“the sun heats the oceans”*
*“heat the water on the stove”*
2. *(slang, transitive)* **發情** — To arouse, to excite (sexually).
3. *(transitive)* **加熱 /加熱** — To cause an increase in temperature of (an object or space); to cause to become hot (often with "up").
4. **加熱** — provide with heat
*“heat the house”*
5. **供熱** — gain heat or get hot
*“The room heated up quickly”*
6. arouse or excite feelings and passions
*“The ostentatious way of living of the rich ignites the hatred of the poor”*
*“The refugees' fate stirred up compassion around the world”*
*n.* 1. **激昂, 激動** — the trait of being intensely emotional
2. **動情週期, 動情期, 發情** — applies to nonhuman mammals: a state or period of heightened sexual arousal and activity

4. *(countable, uncountable)* **熱 /熱** — The condition or quality of being hot.
5. **供暖系統, 供暖設備, 暖氣設備, 暖氣** — utility to warm a building
*“the heating system wasn't working”*
*“they have radiant heating”*

## heave

*v.* 1. **洶湧, 象巨浪般洶湧, 奔騰** — rise and move, as in waves or billows
*“The army surged forward”*
2. **行駛** — move or cause to move in a specified way, direction, or position
*“The vessel hove into sight”*
3. **喘息, 氣促, 氣喘吁吁, 喘氣** — breathe noisily, as when one is exhausted
*“The runners reached the finish line, panting heavily”*
4. **彎曲, 起皺** — bend out of shape, as under pressure or from heat
*“The highway buckled during the heat wave”*
5. utter a sound, as with obvious effort
*“She heaved a deep sigh when she saw the list of things to do”*
6. throw with great effort
*n.* 1. an upward movement (especially a rhythmical rising and falling)
*“the heaving of waves on a rough sea”*
2. (geology) a horizontal dislocation
3. the act of lifting something with great effort
4. an involuntary spasm of ineffectual vomiting
*“a bad case of the heaves”*
5. the act of raising something
*“he responded with a lift of his eyebrow”*
*“fireman learn several different raises for getting ladders up”*
6. throwing something heavy (with great effort)
*“he gave it a mighty heave”*
*“he was not good at heaving passes”*

*“a heavy load”*
*“lead is a heavy metal”*

*“Iago is the heavy role in `Othello'”*

*“dense smoke”*
*“heavy fog”*
5. **厚+的, 喝醉酒+的** — of relatively large extent and density
*“a heavy line”*
6. **笨重+的** — made of fabric having considerable thickness
*“a heavy coat”*

2. a serious (or tragic) role in a play

*“time hung heavy on their hands”*

## hedge

**發音：** /ˈhɛd͡ʒ/
*n.* 1. **灌木樹籬, 籬牆, 樹籬, 籬笆** — a fence formed by a row of closely planted shrubs or bushes
2. **籬牆 /籬牆, 樹籬 /樹籬** — A thicket of bushes or other shrubbery, especially one planted as a fence between two portions of land, or to separate the parts of a garden.
3. **屏障** — A barrier (often consisting of a line of persons or objects) to protect someone or something from harm.
4. **套頭交易, 平衡交易** — any technique designed to reduce or eliminate financial risk; for example, taking two positions that will offset each other if prices change
5. **含糊的話 /含糊的話** — A noncommittal or intentionally ambiguous statement.
6. **對沖 /對沖** — Contract or arrangement reducing one's exposure to risk (for example the risk of price movements or interest rate movements).
*v.* 1. avoid or try to avoid fulfilling, answering, or performing (duties, questions, or issues)
*“He dodged the issue”*
*“she skirted the problem”*
2. hinder or restrict with or as if with a hedge
*“The animals were hedged in”*
3. enclose or bound in with or as it with a hedge or hedges
*“hedge the property”*
4. minimize loss or risk
*“diversify your financial portfolio to hedge price risks”*
*“hedge your bets”*

*“heedful of the warnings”*

<a id="entry-h-001291"></a>
## heel

*n.* 1. **踵, 鞋後跟** — the bottom of a shoe or boot; the back part of a shoe or boot that touches the ground and provides elevation
2. **後跟, 腳後跟, 腳跟, 踵** — the back part of the human foot
3. **桅腳, 桅跟** — the lower end of a ship's mast
4. **尾端部位** — (golf) the part of the clubhead where it joins the shaft
5. someone who is morally reprehensible
*“you dirty dog”*
6. one of the crusty ends of a loaf of bread
*v.* 1. **傾斜** — tilt to one side
*“The balloon heeled over”*
*“the wind made the vessel heel”*
2. **緊隨** — follow at the heels of a person
3. perform with the heels
*“heel that dance”*
4. strike with the heel of the club
*“heel a golf ball”*
5. put a new heel on
*“heel shoes”*

<a id="entry-h-001292"></a>

## height

**發音：** /haɪt/
*n.* 1. **極點, 頂點** — the highest level or degree attainable; the highest stage of development
*“his landscapes were deemed the acme of beauty”*
*“the artist's gifts are at their acme”*
2. **海拔, 高度** — elevation especially above sea level or above the earth's surface
*“the altitude gave her a headache”*
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **身高, 身長 /身長, 體高 /體高** — The vertical distance from the ground to the highest part of a standing person or animal (withers in the case of a horse).
4. the vertical dimension of extension; distance from the base of something to the top
5. (of a standing person) the distance from head to foot

<a id="entry-h-001309"></a>
## heighten

**發音：** /ˈhaɪ̯.tən/
*v.* 1. **提升, 增高, 加強, 提高** — become more extreme
*“The tension heightened”*
2. **使更顯著, 加強, 增強, 增加** — make more extreme; raise in quantity, degree, or intensity
*“heightened interest”*
3. **提高** — To make high; to raise higher; to elevate.
4. **加高, 增高, 提高** — increase the height of
*“The athletes kept jumping over the steadily heightened bars”*
5. **使敏銳** — make (one's senses) more acute
*“This drug will sharpen your vision”*
6. increase
*“This will enhance your enjoyment”*
*“heighten the tension”*

## Helmholtz free energy

*n.* 1. **亥姆霍茲自由能 /亥姆霍茲自由能** — A measure of the useful work obtainable from a constant temperature, constant volume thermodynamic system; more precisely, the difference between internal energy (of a system) and the product of its absolute temperature and entropy.

## help

**發音：** /hɛlp/
*v.* 1. **幫, 協助, 幫忙, 援助, 幫助** — give help or assistance; be of service
*“Everyone helped out during the earthquake”*
*“Can you help me carry this table?”*
2. **有用, 幫忙, 幫助, 起作用** — be of use
*“This will help to prevent accidents”*
3. **能控制, 能自已** — abstain from doing; always used with a negative
*“I can't help myself--I have to smoke”*
*“She could not help watching the sad spectacle”*
4. *(transitive)* **幫助 /幫助, 幫忙 /幫忙** — To provide assistance to (someone or something).
5. **助長, 幫助, 促進, 有用** — contribute to the furtherance of
*“This money will help the development of literacy in developing countries”*
6. improve the condition of
*“These pills will help the patient”*
*n.* 1. **幫助, 幫, 幫忙, 援助** — the activity of contributing to the fulfillment of a need or furtherance of an effort or purpose
*“he gave me an assist with the housework”*
*“could not walk without assistance”*
2. **助理, 幫助者, 幫手, 助手** — a person who contributes to the fulfillment of a need or furtherance of an effort or purpose
*“my invaluable assistant”*
*“they hired additional help to finish the work”*
3. *(uncountable, usually)* **助手, 助理, 副手** — Something or someone which provides assistance with a task.
4. *(uncountable, usually)* **幫 /幫, 幫助 /幫助** — Action given to provide assistance; aid.
5. a resource
*“visual aids in teaching”*
6. a means of serving
*“of no avail”*
*“there's no help for it”*
*interj.* 1. **救命** — A cry of distress or an urgent request for assistance.

*“She could not help watching the sad spectacle”*

4. **半球, 大腦半球** — either half of the cerebrum
5. **半球** — Any half-sphere, formed by a plane intersecting the center of a sphere. .

<a id="entry-h-001482"></a>

<a id="entry-h-001484"></a>

<a id="entry-h-001485"></a>
## hemlock

**發音：** /ˈhɛmˌlɒk/
*n.* 1. **斑毒芹, 毒人參, 毒參, 走馬芹, 野芹** — large branching biennial herb native to Eurasia and Africa and adventive in North America having large fernlike leaves and white flowers; usually found in damp habitats; all parts extremely poisonous
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **鐵杉 /鐵杉** — Any of several coniferous trees, of the genus Tsuga, that grow in North America; the wood of such trees.
3. poisonous drug derived from an Eurasian plant of the genus Conium
*“Socrates refused to flee and died by drinking hemlock”*
4. soft coarse splintery wood of a hemlock tree especially the western hemlock
5. an evergreen tree

<a id="entry-h-001486"></a>
## hemlock water dropwort

*n.* 1. **毒堇** — European poisonous herb having tuberous roots, yellow juice that stains the skin, yellow flowers and foliage resembling celery; all parts extremely poisonous

<a id="entry-h-001487"></a>
## hemming-stitch

*n.* 1. **縫邊** — a stitch used in sewing hems on skirts and dresses

<a id="entry-h-001488"></a>
## hemochromatosis

**發音：** /ˌhiːmə(ʊ)ˌkɹəʊməˈtəʊsɪs/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **血色沉著病, 血色沉著病, 血色病, 青銅色糖尿病 /青銅色糖尿病** — A metabolic disorder causing iron deposits in the body.
2. **血色沉著病, 血色病, 血色素沉著, 青銅色糖尿病, 血色沉著病** — pathology in which iron accumulates in the tissues; characterized by bronzed skin and enlarged liver and diabetes mellitus and abnormalities of the pancreas and the joints

<a id="entry-h-001489"></a>
## hemodialysis

**發音：** /ˌhi.moʊ.daɪˈæ.lɪ.sɪs/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **洗腎 /洗腎, 血液透析** — Dialysis that uses an extracorporeal apparatus to remove waste products from the blood in the case of kidney failure.
2. dialysis of the blood to remove toxic substances or metabolic wastes from the bloodstream; used in the case of kidney failure

<a id="entry-h-001490"></a>
## hemodialyzer

*n.* 1. **人工腎, 人造腎臟, 血液透析器** — a machine that uses dialysis to remove impurities and waste products from the bloodstream before returning the blood to the patient's body

<a id="entry-h-001491"></a>
## hemodynamics

**發音：** /ˌhimoʊdaɪˈnæmɪks/
*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **血流動力學 /血流動力學, 血液動力學 /血液動力學** — The circulation and movement of blood in the body, and the forces involved therein.
2. the branch of physiology that studies the circulation of the blood and the forces involved

<a id="entry-h-001492"></a>

<a id="entry-h-001493"></a>
## hemogenesis

*n.* 1. **生血作用, 血產生, 血液生成, 血生成, 造血** — the formation of blood cells in the living body (especially in the bone marrow)

<a id="entry-h-001494"></a>
## hemoglobin

**發音：** /ˌhimoʊˈɡloʊbɪn/
*n.* 1. **血紅蛋白, 血色素** — a hemoprotein composed of globin and heme that gives red blood cells their characteristic color; function primarily to transport oxygen from the lungs to the body tissues
*“fish have simpler hemoglobin than mammals”*
2. *(US, countable, uncountable)* **血紅蛋白 /血紅蛋白** — The iron-containing substance in red blood cells that transports oxygen from the lungs to the rest of the body; it consists of a protein (globulin), and haem (a porphyrin ring with an atom of iron at its centre).

<a id="entry-h-001495"></a>
## hemoglobinemia

*n.* 1. **血紅蛋白血** — presence of excessive hemoglobin in the blood plasma

<a id="entry-h-001496"></a>
## hemoglobinopathy

*n.* 1. **血紅蛋白病** — a blood disease characterized by the presence of abnormal hemoglobins in the blood

<a id="entry-h-001497"></a>
## hemoglobinuria

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **血紅素尿症 /血紅素尿症** — The presence of hemoglobin in the urine.
2. **血紅蛋白尿** — presence of hemoglobin in the urine

<a id="entry-h-001498"></a>
## hemolymph

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **血淋巴** — A circulating fluid in the bodies of some invertebrates that is the equivalent of blood.

<a id="entry-h-001499"></a>
## hemolysis

*n.* 1. **溶血, 血細胞溶解** — lysis of erythrocytes with the release of hemoglobin

<a id="entry-h-001500"></a>
## hemolytic

*adj.* 1. **溶血+的** — relating to or involving or causing hemolysis
*“hemolytic anemia”*

<a id="entry-h-001501"></a>
## hemolytic anemia

*n.* 1. **溶血性貧血** — anemia resulting from destruction of erythrocytes

<a id="entry-h-001502"></a>

*n.* 1. **血友病** — congenital tendency to uncontrolled bleeding; usually affects males and is transmitted from mother to son

<a id="entry-h-001503"></a>
## hemophilia a

*n.* 1. **A型血友病, 甲型血友病** — hemophilia caused by a congenital deficiency of factor VIII; occurs almost exclusively in men

<a id="entry-h-001504"></a>
## hemophilia b

*n.* 1. **B型血友病, 乙型血友病** — a clotting disorder similar to hemophilia A but caused by a congenital deficiency of factor IX

<a id="entry-h-001505"></a>
## hemophilic

*adj.* 1. **患血友病+的, 血友病+的** — relating to or having hemophilia

<a id="entry-h-001506"></a>

*n.* 1. **生血作用, 血產生, 血液生成, 血生成, 造血** — the formation of blood cells in the living body (especially in the bone marrow)

*“hemopoietic stem cells in bone marrow”*

<a id="entry-h-001508"></a>

*n.* 1. **血紅素蛋白** — a conjugated protein linked to a compound of iron and porphyrin

<a id="entry-h-001509"></a>

**發音：** /ˈhɛm(ə)ɹɪd͡ʒ/
*n.* 1. **出血** — the flow of blood from a ruptured blood vessel
2. *(US, countable, uncountable)* **出血** — A heavy release of blood within or from the body.
*v.* 1. **出血, 流血** — lose blood from one's body

## hence

**發音：** /ˈhɛns/
*adv.* 1. *(archaic, not-comparable)* **從這裡 /從這裡, 從這兒 /從這兒** — From here, from this place, away.
2. *(conjunctive, not-comparable)* **所以, 因此, 於是 /於是** — As a result; therefore, for this reason.
3. (used to introduce a logical conclusion) from that fact or reason or as a result
*“therefore X must be true”*
*“the eggs were fresh and hence satisfactory”*
4. from this place
*“get thee hence!”*
5. from this time
*“a year hence it will be forgotten”*

<a id="entry-h-001540"></a>
## henceforth

**發音：** /hɛnsˈfɔɹθ/

## henry

2. **亨利** — a unit of inductance in which an induced electromotive force of one volt is produced when the current is varied at the rate of one ampere per second

## hepatitis

2. *(countable, uncountable)* **肝炎** — Inflammation of the liver, sometimes caused by a viral infection.

<a id="entry-h-001569"></a>
## hepatitis A

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **甲型肝炎, 甲肝, A型肝炎, A肝** — An acute form of hepatitis, transmitted by the orofecal route.

<a id="entry-h-001570"></a>
## hepatitis a

*n.* 1. **A肝炎, 甲型肝炎** — an acute but benign form of viral hepatitis caused by an RNA virus that does not persist in the blood serum and is usually transmitted by ingesting food or drink that is contaminated with fecal matter

<a id="entry-h-001571"></a>
## hepatitis B

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **乙型肝炎, 乙肝, B型肝炎, B肝** — An acute form of hepatitis, transmitted by blood, sexual contact, and body fluids.

<a id="entry-h-001572"></a>
## hepatitis b

*n.* 1. **B肝炎** — an acute (sometimes fatal) form of viral hepatitis caused by a DNA virus that tends to persist in the blood serum and is transmitted by sexual contact or by transfusion or by ingestion of contaminated blood or other bodily fluids

<a id="entry-h-001573"></a>
## hepatitis C

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **丙型肝炎, 丙肝, C型肝炎, C肝** — An acute form of hepatitis, transmitted by blood, sexual contact, and body fluids.

<a id="entry-h-001574"></a>
## hepatitis c

*n.* 1. **丙肝, 肝炎** — a viral hepatitis clinically indistinguishable from hepatitis B but caused by a single-stranded RNA virus; usually transmitted by parenteral means (as injection of an illicit drug or blood transfusion or exposure to blood or blood products)

<a id="entry-h-001575"></a>
## hepatitis D

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **丁型肝炎, 丁肝, D型肝炎, D肝** — An acute form of hepatitis, transmitted by blood, sexual contact, and body fluids, and caused by the hepatitis delta virus.

<a id="entry-h-001576"></a>
## hepatitis delta

*n.* 1. **乙肝** — a severe form of hepatitis

<a id="entry-h-001577"></a>
## hepatitis E

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **戊型肝炎, 戊肝, E型肝炎, E肝** — An acute form of hepatitis, transmitted by the orofecal route.

<a id="entry-h-001637"></a>
## herd

**發音：** /hɜːd/
*n.* 1. **獸群, 牧群, 群** — a group of cattle or sheep or other domestic mammals all of the same kind that are herded by humans
2. *(collective)* **群** — A number of domestic animals assembled together under the watch or ownership of a keeper.
3. *(collective)* **獸群 /獸群, 群** — Any collection of animals gathered or travelling in a company.
4. *(derogatory, usually)* **群眾 /群眾** — A crowd, a mass of people or things; a rabble.
5. a group of wild mammals of one species that remain together: antelope or elephants or seals or whales or zebra
6. a crowd especially of ordinary or undistinguished persons or things
*“his brilliance raised him above the ruck”*
*“the children resembled a fairy herd”*
*v.* 1. **群集** — move together, like a herd
2. **放牧** — keep, move, or drive animals
*“Who will be herding the cattle when the cowboy dies?”*
3. cause to herd, drive, or crowd together
*“We herded the children into a spare classroom”*

<a id="entry-h-001638"></a>
## herd immunity

**發音：** /ˈhəːd ɪˈmjuːnɪti/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **群體免疫 /群體免疫** — The indirect protection against the spread of a contagious disease in a given population caused by the immunity of a significant proportion of the population to that particular disease; the immunity may have been obtained through having survived the infection or through vaccination.

3. someone who drives a herd

## herdsman

2. someone who drives a herd

<a id="entry-h-001642"></a>
## here

**發音：** /ˈhɪə̯/
*adv.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **到這兒 /到這兒** — To this place; used in place of the literary or archaic hither.
2. in or at this place; where the speaker or writer is
*“I work here”*
*“turn here”*
3. in this circumstance or respect or on this point or detail
*“what do we have here?”*
*“here I must disagree”*
4. to this place (especially toward the speaker)
*“come here, please”*
5. at this time; now
*“we'll adjourn here for lunch and discuss the remaining issues this afternoon”*
*adj.* 1. being here now
*“is everyone here?”*
*n.* 1. the present location; this place
*“where do we go from here?”*
2. queen of the Olympian gods in ancient Greek mythology; sister and wife of Zeus remembered for her jealously of the many mortal women Zeus fell in love with; identified with Roman Juno

<a id="entry-h-001643"></a>
## here and now

*n.* 1. **目前, 現在** — at this time
*“the disappointments of the here and now”*
*“she is studying at the moment”*
*adv.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **此時此地** — At this time and in this place.

<a id="entry-h-001644"></a>
## here and there

*adv.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **到處 /到處, 滿處 /滿處** — In, at or to various places; in one place and another.
2. in or to various places; first this place and then that
*“he worked here and there but never for long in one town”*
*“we drove here and there in the darkness”*

<a id="entry-h-001645"></a>
## here be dragons

*phr.* 1. **此處有龍 /此處有龍** — A fanciful notation, commonly attributed to historical maps, held to indicate either the belief that unknown dangers exist in a certain location on the map, or that actual dragons can be found there.

<a id="entry-h-001646"></a>
## here goes nothing

*interj.* 1. *(idiomatic)* **管不了那麼多了 /管不了那麼多了** — Indicates a lack of confidence or certainty about the activity about to be tried, but that it will be attempted nonetheless.

<a id="entry-h-001647"></a>
## here we go again

*interj.* 1. *(idiomatic)* **又來了 /又來了** — An expression of frustration upon seeing or hearing something bad repeated.

<a id="entry-h-001648"></a>
## here you are

*phr.* 1. *(idiomatic)* **給你 /給你, 請拿好 /請拿好** — Said when handing something over to someone or doing a favour to them, usually to draw the recipient's attention to the exchange; equivalent to “thank you” when receiving something.

<a id="entry-h-001649"></a>
## hereafter

**發音：** /hɪɹˈæftɚ/
*adv.* 1. **在下文, 在下文中** — in a subsequent part of this document or statement or matter etc.
*“the landlord demises unto the tenant the premises hereinafter called the demised premises”*
*“the terms specified hereunder”*
2. *(dated, not-comparable)* **此後 /此後** — In time to come; in some future time or state.
3. in a future life or state
*“hope to win salvation hereafter”*
4. following this in time or order or place; after this
*“hereafter you will no longer receive an allowance”*
*n.* 1. **後世, 來世, 來生** — life after death
2. **今後, 以後, 未來, 將來** — the time yet to come

<a id="entry-h-001650"></a>
## hereditament

*n.* 1. **可繼承的財產, 不動產** — any property (real or personal or mixed) that can be inherited

<a id="entry-h-001651"></a>
## hereditary

**發音：** /həˈɹɛdɪt(ə)ɹi/
*adj.* 1. **遺傳的 /遺傳的, 遺留的 /遺留的** — Passed on as an inheritance, by last will or intestate.
2. **遺傳+的** — occurring among members of a family usually by heredity
*“an inherited disease”*
*“familial traits”*
3. **世襲的 /世襲的** — Of a title, honor or right: legally granted to somebody's descendant after that person's death.
4. **祖傳+的** — inherited or inheritable by established rules (usually legal rules) of descent
*“ancestral home”*
*“ancestral lore”*
5. **遺傳的 /遺傳的** — Of a disease or trait: passed from a parent to offspring in the genes.

## hereditary condition

<a id="entry-h-001653"></a>
## hereditary disease

*n.* 1. **遺傳病** — a disease or disorder that is inherited genetically

<a id="entry-h-001654"></a>
## hereditary motor and sensory neuropathy

*n.* 1. **腓骨肌萎縮徵** — a form of neuropathy that can begin between childhood and young adulthood; characterized by weakness and atrophy of the muscles of the hands and lower legs; progression is slow and individuals affected can have a normal life span; inheritance is X-linked recessive or X-linked dominant

<a id="entry-h-001655"></a>
## heredity

**發音：** /hɪˈɹɛdɪti/
*n.* 1. **遺傳** — the biological process whereby genetic factors are transmitted from one generation to the next
2. *(uncountable, usually)* **遺傳 /遺傳** — Hereditary transmission of the physical and genetic qualities of parents to their offspring; the biological law by which living beings tend to repeat their characteristics in their descendants.
3. the total of inherited attributes

*“the landlord demises unto the tenant the premises hereinafter called the demised premises”*

**發音：** /hɛˈɹiːzɪɑːk/
*n.* 1. **異端領袖 /異端領袖, 異端頭目 /異端頭目** — The founder of a heresy, or a major ecclesiastical proponent of such a heresy.

<a id="entry-h-001662"></a>
## heresy

**發音：** /ˈhɛɹəsi/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **異端 /異端, 邪說 /邪說, 異教 /異教, 邪教** — A doctrine held by a member of a religion at variance or conflict with established religious beliefs.
2. any opinions or doctrines at variance with the official or orthodox position
3. a belief that rejects the orthodox tenets of a religion

<a id="entry-h-001663"></a>
## heretic

**發音：** /ˈhɛɹɪtɪk/
*n.* 1. **異端 /異端** — Someone whose beliefs are contrary to the fundamental tenets of a religion they claim to belong to.
2. a person who holds religious beliefs in conflict with the dogma of the Roman Catholic Church
3. a person who holds unorthodox opinions in any field (not merely religion)

*“the terms specified hereunder”*
2. under the terms of this agreement

## heritability

*n.* 1. *(countable)* **遺傳力 /遺傳力, 遺傳度 /遺傳度** — The ratio of the genetic variance of a population to its phenotypic variance; i.e. the proportion of variability that is genetic in origin

<a id="entry-h-001667"></a>
## heritable

*adj.* 1. **可繼承+的** — capable of being inherited
*“inheritable traits such as eye color”*
*“an inheritable title”*

<a id="entry-h-001668"></a>
## heritage

**發音：** /ˈhɛɹ.ɪ.tɪd͡ʒ/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **遺產 /遺產** — An inheritance; property that may be inherited.
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **遺產 /遺產** — A tradition; a practice or set of values that is passed down from preceding generations through families or through institutional memory.
3. **繼承財產, 遺產** — that which is inherited; a title or property or estate that passes by law to the heir on the death of the owner
4. practices that are handed down from the past by tradition
*“a heritage of freedom”*
5. any attribute or immaterial possession that is inherited from ancestors
*“my only inheritance was my mother's blessing”*
*“the world's heritage of knowledge”*
6. hereditary succession to a title or an office or property

## hero

**發音：** /ˈhɪə.ɹəʊ/
*n.* 1. **勇士, 英雄** — a man distinguished by exceptional courage and nobility and strength
*“RAF pilots were the heroes of the Battle of Britain”*

3. the principal character in a play or movie or novel or poem
4. someone who fights for a cause
5. Greek mathematician and inventor who devised a way to determine the area of a triangle and who described various mechanical devices (first century)
6. (classical mythology) a being of great strength and courage celebrated for bold exploits; often the offspring of a mortal and a god
*v.* 1. *(informal, intransitive)* **英雄, 勇士, 主角** — To act as a hero (brave person; role model; or protagonist).

## heroic

*adj.* 1. **英勇+的, 英雄+的, 英雄式+的** — relating to or characteristic of heroes of antiquity
*“heroic legends”*
*“the heroic age”*
2. very imposing or impressive; surpassing the ordinary (especially in size or scale)
*“an epic voyage”*
*“of heroic proportions”*
3. having or displaying qualities appropriate for heroes
*“the heroic attack on the beaches of Normandy”*
*“heroic explorers”*
4. of behavior that is impressive and ambitious in scale or scope
*“an expansive lifestyle”*
*“in the grand manner”*
5. showing extreme courage; especially of actions courageously undertaken in desperation as a last resort
*“made a last desperate attempt to reach the climber”*
*“the desperate gallantry of our naval task forces marked the turning point in the Pacific war"- G.C.Marshall”*
*n.* 1. a verse form suited to the treatment of heroic or elevated themes; dactylic hexameter or iambic pentameter

## hesitancy

*n.* 1. **不情願, 猶豫, 躊躇, 遲疑** — a certain degree of unwillingness
*“a reluctance to commit himself”*
*“his hesitancy revealed his basic indisposition”*
2. a feeling of diffidence and indecision about doing something

<a id="entry-h-001743"></a>
## hesitate

**發音：** /ˈhɛz.ɪ.teɪt/
*v.* 1. **不願, 猶豫, 動搖, 猶豫不決, 躊躇** — pause or hold back in uncertainty or unwillingness
*“Authorities hesitate to quote exact figures”*
2. **停頓, 暫停** — interrupt temporarily an activity before continuing
*“The speaker paused”*
3. *(intransitive)* **猶豫 /猶豫, 躊躇 /躊躇, 遲疑 /遲疑** — To stop or pause respecting decision or action; to be in suspense or uncertainty as to a determination.

<a id="entry-h-001744"></a>
## hesitation

**發音：** /ˌhɛzɪˈteɪʃən/
*n.* 1. **不情願, 猶豫, 躊躇, 遲疑** — a certain degree of unwillingness
*“a reluctance to commit himself”*
*“his hesitancy revealed his basic indisposition”*
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **躊躇 /躊躇, 猶豫 /猶豫, 遲疑 /遲疑, 彷徨** — An act of hesitating
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **支吾, 吞吐, 口吃, 結巴 /結巴** — A faltering in speech; stammering.
4. indecision in speech or action
5. the act of pausing uncertainly
*“there was a hesitation in his speech”*

2. **冬眠** — be in an inactive or dormant state
3. *(intransitive)* **休眠** — To enter a standby state which conserves power without losing the contents of memory.

*“Bears must eat a lot of food before they hibernate in their caves”*

3. **避寒, 冬眠, 過冬** — cessation from or slowing of activity during the winter; especially slowing of metabolism in some animals
4. the act of retiring into inactivity
*“he emerged from his hibernation to make his first appearance in several years”*

## hiccough

*“how do you cure the hiccups?”*
*v.* 1. **打嗝, 打嗝兒** — breathe spasmodically, and make a sound
*“When you have to hiccup, drink a glass of cold water”*

<a id="entry-h-001852"></a>
## hiccup

**發音：** /ˈhɪkʌp/
*n.* 1. **呃逆, 打嗝, 打嗝兒** — (usually plural) the state of having reflex spasms of the diaphragm accompanied by a rapid closure of the glottis producing an audible sound; sometimes a symptom of indigestion

*“Probably his horse would be close to where he was hiding”*

6. make undecipherable or imperceptible by obscuring or concealing
*“a hidden message”*
*“a veiled threat”*

## high

*adj.* 1. **高+的** — greater than normal in degree or intensity or amount
*“a high temperature”*
*“a high price”*
2. **顯赫+的, 突出+的, 高級+的, 傑出+的** — standing above others in quality or position
*“people in high places”*
*“the high priest”*
3. **感覺自在輕鬆的** — slightly and pleasantly intoxicated from alcohol or a drug (especially marijuana)
4. (literal meaning) being at or having a relatively great or specific elevation or upward extension (sometimes used in combinations like `knee-high')
*“a high mountain”*
*“high ceilings”*
5. used of sounds and voices; high in pitch or frequency
6. happy and excited and energetic
*adv.* 1. **離地面高+地, 高+地, 高處+地** — at a great altitude
*“he climbed high on the ladder”*
2. **高+地** — in or to a high position, amount, or degree
*“prices have gone up far too high”*
3. **奢侈+地, 豐富+地, 富裕+地, 豪華+地** — in a rich manner
*“he lives high”*
4. **向高處, 在高處, 高+地** — far up toward the source
*“he lives high up the river”*
*n.* 1. **高壓氣團** — an air mass of higher than normal pressure
*“the east coast benefits from a Bermuda high”*
2. **得意洋洋, 欣快** — a state of sustained elation
*“I'm on a permanent high these days”*
3. **恍惚狀態** — a state of altered consciousness induced by alcohol or narcotics
*“they took drugs to get a high on”*
4. **高速檔** — a forward gear with a gear ratio that gives the greatest vehicle velocity for a given engine speed
5. a lofty level or position or degree
*“summer temperatures reached an all-time high”*
6. a high place
*“they stood on high and observed the countryside”*
*“he doesn't like heights”*

## hinder

**發音：** /ˈhɪndəː/
*v.* 1. **妨礙, 阻止, 阻礙** — be a hindrance or obstacle to
*“She is impeding the progress of our project”*
2. **妨礙, 阻塞, 攔阻, 阻礙** — hinder or prevent the progress or accomplishment of
*“His brother blocked him at every turn”*
3. **妨礙, 阻止, 阻礙** — put at a disadvantage
*“The brace I have to wear is hindering my movements”*
4. *(transitive)* **妨礙 /妨礙, 阻礙 /阻礙** — To make difficult to accomplish; to act as an obstacle; to frustrate.
5. *(ambitransitive)* **妨礙 /妨礙, 阻礙 /阻礙** — To delay or impede; to keep back, to prevent.
*adj.* 1. *(comparative, form-of)* **後面 /後面** — comparative form of hind: more hind
2. located at or near the back of an animal
*“back (or hind) legs”*
*“the hinder part of a carcass”*

<a id="entry-h-002162"></a>

**發音：** /hɪˈstɒɹɪkəl/

*“historical scholars”*
*“a historical perspective”*
2. **歷史上+的, 歷史+的** — having once lived or existed or taken place in the real world as distinct from being legendary
*“the historical Jesus”*
*“doubt that a historical Camelot every existed”*
3. **歷史的 /歷史的** — Of, concerning, or in accordance with recorded history, (particularly) as opposed to legends, myths, and fictions.
4. **歷史+的, 史實+的** — used of the study of a phenomenon (especially language) as it changes through time
*“diachronic linguistics”*

*“historical (or historic) times”*

<a id="entry-h-002173"></a>
## history

*n.* 1. **歷史, 史, 過去發生的事, 過去** — the aggregate of past events
*“a critical time in the school's history”*
2. **歷史記錄, 記事, 記錄, 歷史, 經歷** — a record or narrative description of past events
*“a history of France”*
*“he gave an inaccurate account of the plot to kill the president”*
3. **歷史學** — the discipline that records and interprets past events involving human beings
*“he teaches Medieval history”*
*“history takes the long view”*
4. the continuum of events occurring in succession leading from the past to the present and even into the future
*“all of human history”*
5. all that is remembered of the past as preserved in writing; a body of knowledge
*“the dawn of recorded history”*
*“from the beginning of history”*

<a id="entry-h-002177"></a>
## hit

*v.* 1. **使撞擊, 擊中, 撞倒, 碰上, 碰擊** — hit against; come into sudden contact with
*“The car hit a tree”*
*“He struck the table with his elbow”*
2. **擊, 打擊, 打** — deal a blow to, either with the hand or with an instrument
*“He hit her hard in the face”*
3. **到達, 抵達, 達到** — reach a destination, either real or abstract
*“We hit Detroit by noon”*
*“The water reached the doorstep”*
4. **偶然碰上** — encounter by chance
*“I stumbled across a long-lost cousin last night in a restaurant”*
5. **殘殺, 謀殺, 殺, 殺害** — kill intentionally and with premeditation
*“The mafia boss ordered his enemies murdered”*
6. **衝撞, 擊, 擊打, 打擊, 撞** — drive something violently into a location
*“he hit his fist on the table”*
*“she struck her head on the low ceiling”*
*n.* 1. (baseball) a successful stroke in an athletic contest (especially in baseball)
*“he came all the way around on Williams' hit”*
2. the act of contacting one thing with another
*“repeated hitting raised a large bruise”*
*“after three misses she finally got a hit”*
3. a conspicuous success
*“that song was his first hit and marked the beginning of his career”*
*“that new Broadway show is a real smasher”*
4. (physics) a brief event in which two or more bodies come together
*“the collision of the particles resulted in an exchange of energy and a change of direction”*
5. a dose of a narcotic drug
6. a murder carried out by an underworld syndicate
*“it has all the earmarks of a Mafia hit”*

2. *(countable, uncountable)* **打帶跑 /打帶跑** — A play where the baserunners leave their base before the batter hits the ball, assuming that the batter will in fact hit the ball and this will give them an advantage.
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **一擊脫離 /一擊脫離** — The tactic of attacking one place and then quickly moving to another before defenders can react.
*v.* 1. **肇事逃逸, 撞人逃逸** — To join a poker game, quickly win several hands, then leave.

## hoist

**發音：** /hɔɪst/
*v.* 1. *(transitive)* **提升, 升起, 吊起** — To raise; to lift; to elevate (especially, to raise or lift to a desired elevation, by means of tackle or pulley, said of a sail, a flag, a heavy package or weight).
2. *(figuratively, often, transitive)* **舉起 /舉起** — To lift a trophy or similar prize into the air in celebration of a victory.
3. *(historical, transitive)* **吊打** — To lift someone up to be flogged.
4. *(intransitive)* **被吊起, 被升起** — To be lifted up.
5. raise or haul up with or as if with mechanical help
*“hoist the bicycle onto the roof of the car”*
6. move from one place to another by lifting
*“They hoisted the patient onto the operating table”*
*n.* 1. **捲揚機, 吊升機械, 起重機** — lifting device for raising heavy or cumbersome objects
2. **捲揚機 /捲揚機, 起重機 /起重機, 起吊, 吊升** — The act of hoisting; a lift.
3. **升至最高位置** — The triangular vertical position of a flag, as opposed to the flying state, or triangular vertical position of a sail, when flying from a mast.
4. **縱帆頂帆升起位置 /縱帆頂帆升起位置** — The position of a main fore-and-aft topsail on a ship and fore fore-and-aft topsail on a ship.

<a id="entry-h-002306"></a>
## hold

*v.* 1. **使保持某狀態, 維持, 保持** — keep in a certain state, position, or activity; e.g., "keep clean"
*“hold in place”*
*“She always held herself as a lady”*
2. **持有, 佔有, 掌握, 擁有, 有** — have or possess, either in a concrete or an abstract sense
*“She has $1,000 in the bank”*
*“He has got two beautiful daughters”*
3. **保留, 保持** — secure and keep for possible future use or application
*“The landlord retained the security deposit”*
*“I reserve the right to disagree”*
4. **持, 佔有, 掌握** — have rightfully; of rights, titles, and offices
*“She bears the title of Duchess”*
*“He held the governorship for almost a decade”*
5. **托住, 承受住, 支承, 支撐, 支撐住** — be the physical support of; carry the weight of
*“The beam holds up the roof”*
*“He supported me with one hand while I balanced on the beam”*
6. **包含, 含, 含有, 容納** — contain or hold; have within
*“The jar carries wine”*
*“The canteen holds fresh water”*
*n.* 1. **掌握** — understanding of the nature or meaning or quality or magnitude of something
*“he has a good grasp of accounting practices”*
2. **延遲, 耽擱, 耽擱的時間** — time during which some action is awaited
*“instant replay caused too long a delay”*
*“he ordered a hold in the action”*
3. **扣押, 拘留, 監禁** — a state of being confined (usually for a short time)
*“his detention was politically motivated”*
*“the prisoner is on hold”*
4. **要塞, 堡壘** — a stronghold
5. **牢房** — a cell in a jail or prison
6. **手柄, 把柄, 把手, 柄** — the appendage to an object that is designed to be held in order to use or move it
*“he grabbed the hammer by the handle”*
*“it was an old briefcase but it still had a good grip”*

<a id="entry-h-002307"></a>
## hold a candle

**發音：** /ˌhəʊ̯ld ə ˈkan.dəl/
*v.* 1. **望其項背** — To compare; to be even remotely of the same quality, skill, etc. as another.

## hold back

*v.* 1. **約束, 限制, 抑制** — keep under control; keep in check
*“suppress a smile”*
*“Keep your temper”*
2. **候, 待, 等待, 等** — wait before acting
*“the scientists held off announcing their results until they repeated the experiment”*
3. **保留, 保持** — secure and keep for possible future use or application
*“The landlord retained the security deposit”*
*“I reserve the right to disagree”*
4. **隱蔽, 隱匿, 隱瞞, 隱藏** — hold back; keep from being perceived by others
*“She conceals her anger well”*
5. hold back, as of a danger or an enemy; check the expansion or influence of
*“Arrest the downward trend”*
*“Check the growth of communism in South East Asia”*
6. refrain from doing
*“she forbore a snicker”*

*“Will this wallpaper adhere to the wall?”*

2. **剋制, 約束, 節制, 遏制, 抑制** — lessen the intensity of; temper; hold in restraint; hold or keep within limits
*“moderate your alcohol intake”*
*“hold your tongue”*
3. **隱蔽, 隱匿, 隱瞞, 隱藏** — hold back; keep from being perceived by others
*“She conceals her anger well”*

*“Dallas had enough of a lead to hold the Broncos off”*

## hold on

*v.* 1. **等一下, 不掛斷電話** — stop and wait, as if awaiting further instructions or developments
*“Hold on a moment!”*
2. **不屈不撓, 堅持不懈, 孜孜不倦, 堅持** — be persistent, refuse to stop
*“he persisted to call me every night”*
*“The child persisted and kept asking questions”*
3. **不掛斷電話** — hold the phone line open
*“Please hang on while I get your folder”*
4. *(idiomatic)* **且慢** — Wait a short while.
5. **保存, 維持, 留下, 保持** — retain possession of
*“Can I keep my old stuffed animals?”*
*“She kept her maiden name after she married”*
6. hold firmly

## hollow

*n.* 1. **窪地** — a cavity or space in something
*“hunger had caused the hollows in their cheeks”*

3. a small valley between mountains
*“he built himself a cabin in a hollow high up in the Appalachians”*
4. a depression hollowed out of solid matter
*adj.* 1. **挖空+的, 中空+的, 空+的** — not solid; having a space or gap or cavity
*“a hollow wall”*
*“a hollow tree”*
2. **空心, 中空, 空** — Having an empty space or cavity inside.
3. *(figuratively)* **空虛 /空虛** — Without substance; having no real or significant worth; meaningless.
4. as if echoing in a hollow space
*“the hollow sound of footsteps in the empty ballroom”*
5. devoid of significance or point
*“empty promises”*
*“a hollow victory”*
*v.* 1. **使變空, 挖空** — remove the inner part or the core of
*“the mining company wants to excavate the hillside”*
2. remove the interior of
*“hollow out a tree trunk”*

<a id="entry-h-002436"></a>
## home

*adv.* 1. at or to or in the direction of one's home or family
*“He stays home on weekends”*
*“after the game the children brought friends home for supper”*
2. on or to the point aimed at
*“the arrow struck home”*
3. to the fullest extent; to the heart
*“drove the nail home”*
*“drove his point home”*
*n.* 1. **住所, 住處, 住宅** — housing that someone is living in
*“he built a modest dwelling near the pond”*
*“they raise money to provide homes for the homeless”*
2. **本壘** — (baseball) base consisting of a rubber slab where the batter stands; it must be touched by a base runner in order to score
*“he ruled that the runner failed to touch home”*
3. **舒適安逸的地方, 避難所** — an environment offering affection and security
*“home is where the heart is”*
*“he grew up in a good Christian home”*
4. **人家, 家庭成員, 家族, 家庭, 戶** — a social unit living together
*“he moved his family to Virginia”*
*“It was a good Christian household”*
5. **休養所, 療養所, 療養院** — an institution where people are cared for
*“a home for the elderly”*
6. where you live at a particular time
*“deliver the package to my home”*
*“he doesn't have a home to go to”*
*adj.* 1. **家+的, 家鄉+的, 故土+的** — relating to or being where one lives or where one's roots are
*“my home town”*
2. used of your own ground
*“a home game”*
3. inside the country
*“the British Home Office has broader responsibilities than the United States Department of the Interior”*
*“the nation's internal politics”*
*v.* 1. **回國, 回窩, 歸巢** — return home accurately from a long distance
*“homing pigeons”*
2. provide with, or send to, a home

## homology

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **同調, 同調論** — A general way of associating a sequence of algebraic objects, such as abelian groups or modules, to a sequence of topological spaces; also used attributively: see Usage notes below.
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **同調, 同調群** — Given a chain complex {Gₙ} and its associated set of homomorphisms {Hₙ}, the rule which explains how each Hₙ maps Gₙ into the kernel of Gₙ₊₁.
3. the quality of being similar or corresponding in position or value or structure or function

## honest

**發音：** /ˈɒn.ɪst/
*adj.* 1. **廉潔+的, 知廉恥+的, 老實+的, 誠實+的, 正直+的** — not disposed to cheat or defraud; not deceptive or fraudulent
*“honest lawyers”*
*“honest reporting”*
2. **直率+的, 誠實+的, 坦率+的, 真誠+的, 誠懇+的** — without dissimulation; frank
*“my honest opinion”*
3. **可信任+的, 可信賴+的, 可靠+的** — worthy of being depended on
*“a dependable worker”*
*“an honest working stiff”*
4. **誠實 /誠實, 正直, 老實 /老實** — Scrupulous with regard to telling the truth; not given to swindling, lying, or fraud; upright.
5. without pretensions
*“worked at an honest trade”*
*“good honest food”*
6. marked by truth
*“gave honest answers”*
*“honest reporting”*

<a id="entry-h-002561"></a>
## honestly

**發音：** /ˈɒn.ɪst.li/
*adv.* 1. **正直地** — In an honest manner.
2. (used as intensives reflecting the speaker's attitude) it is sincerely the case that
*“honestly, I don't believe it”*
*“candidly, I think she doesn't have a conscience”*
3. in an honest manner
*“in he can't get it honestly, he is willing to steal it”*
*“was known for dealing aboveboard in everything”*

## honeycomb

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **蜂窩 /蜂窩, 蜂巢** — A substance made by bees (clade Anthophila) primarily from beeswax which has hexagonal cells to hold their larvae, and for storing pollen and honey to feed the larvae and themselves when other food is scarce; it is also eaten by humans as part of comb-honey; (countable) a single sheet made up of two layers of this substance.

*“The cliffs were honeycombed”*

<a id="entry-h-002613"></a>
## honorary consul

*n.* 1. **名譽領事 /名譽領事** — A person representing a country as a local consul, who is a private person (without diplomatic immunity; not a career diplomat) and part-time representative, with their own primary pursuits, frequently a citizen of the country that is hosting the consul (a local citizen; not a citizen of the country being represented) or expatriate (citizen of the country being represented, living and working in the local country), frequently with commercial ties to the country being represented.

*“our esteemed leader”*
*“a prestigious author”*

<a id="entry-h-002616"></a>

**發音：** /ˌɒnəˈɹɪfɪk/

2. an expression of respect
*“the Japanese use many honorifics”*
*adj.* 1. **給予榮譽+的, 表示敬意+的, 尊敬+的, 表示尊敬+的** — conferring or showing honor or respect
*“honorific social status commonly attaches to membership in a recognized profession”*

<a id="entry-h-002617"></a>
## honour

*n.* 1. **榮譽, 名譽** — the state of being honored
2. **信用, 名譽, 榮譽** — the quality of being honorable and having a good name
*“a man of honor”*
3. a tangible symbol signifying approval or distinction
*“an award for bravery”*
4. a woman's virtue or chastity
*v.* 1. **授予榮譽, 授予酬謝, 給以榮譽** — bestow honor or rewards upon
*“Today we honor our soldiers”*
*“The scout was rewarded for courageous action”*
2. **尊敬, 尊重** — show respect towards
*“honor your parents!”*
3. **承兌** — accept as pay
*“we honor checks and drafts”*

<a id="entry-h-002618"></a>
## honour killing

*n.* 1. *(euphemistic)* **名譽殺害 /名譽殺害, 榮譽殺害 /榮譽殺害, 名譽殺人 /名譽殺人, 榮譽謀殺 /榮譽謀殺** — A typically Middle Eastern and South Asian cultural practice whereby a family member, usually female, is murdered by other family members, the act being justified as retribution for a (usually sexual) slight upon the family's honour.

<a id="entry-h-002619"></a>

*“led an honorable life”*
2. **值得尊敬+的, 合乎道德+的, 品性端正+的** — adhering to ethical and moral principles

<a id="entry-h-002682"></a>
## hope

**發音：** /ˈhəʊp/
*v.* 1. **希望, 盼望, 指望, 冀望** — To want (something) to happen, with a sense of expectation that it might [with that (+ clause); or (informal) with clause; or with so or (negative) not].
2. expect and wish
*“I trust you will behave better from now on”*
*“I hope she understands that she cannot expect a raise”*
3. be optimistic; be full of hope; have hopes
*“I am still hoping that all will turn out well”*
4. intend with some possibility of fulfilment
*“I hope to have finished this work by tomorrow evening”*
*n.* 1. **希望** — the general feeling that some desire will be fulfilled
*“in spite of his troubles he never gave up hope”*
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **希望, 指望, 盼望** — The feeling of trust, confidence, belief or expectation that something wished for can or will happen.
3. *(countable)* **願望 /願望** — A person or thing that is a source of hope.
4. a specific instance of feeling hopeful
*“it revived their hope of winning the pennant”*
5. grounds for feeling hopeful about the future
*“there is little or no promise that he will recover”*
6. someone (or something) on which expectations are centered
*“he was their best hope for a victory”*

<a id="entry-h-002683"></a>
## hope chest

*n.* 1. **嫁妝箱** — chest for storage of clothing (trousseau) and household goods in anticipation of marriage

<a id="entry-h-002684"></a>

<a id="entry-h-002685"></a>
## hopeful

*“a line of people hopeful of obtaining tickets”*
*“found a hopeful way of attacking the problem”*
2. full or promise
*“had a bright future in publishing”*
*“the scandal threatened an abrupt end to a promising political career”*

*“a lofty aspirant”*
*“two executive hopefuls joined the firm”*

2. with hope; in a hopeful manner
*“we searched hopefully for a good position”*
3. it is hoped
*“hopefully the weather will be fine on Sunday”*

<a id="entry-h-002687"></a>

<a id="entry-h-002688"></a>
## hopeless

**發音：** /ˈhəʊplɪs/
*adj.* 1. **沒有希望的 /沒有希望的, 絕望的 /絕望的** — Without hope; despairing; not expecting anything positive.
2. **不行+的, 無能+的, 糟糕+的** — of a person unable to do something skillfully
*“I'm hopeless at mathematics”*
3. without hope because there seems to be no possibility of comfort or success
*“in an agony of hopeless grief”*
*“with a hopeless sigh he sat down”*
4. certain to fail
*“the situation is hopeless”*

<a id="entry-h-002859"></a>
## hostile

**發音：** /ˈhɒstaɪl/
*adj.* 1. **懷敵意+的, 懷有敵意+的, 敵對性質+的, 敵意+的, 不友善+的** — characterized by enmity or ill will
*“a hostile nation”*
*“a hostile remark”*
2. **敵對的 /敵對的, 懷敵意的 /懷敵意的** — Not friendly; appropriate to an enemy; showing the disposition of an enemy; showing ill will and malevolence or a desire to thwart and injure.
3. **不友善+的, 敵對+的, 懷敵意+的, 懷有敵意+的** — impossible to bring into friendly accord
*“hostile factions”*
4. not belonging to your own country's forces or those of an ally
*“hostile naval and air forces”*
5. very unfavorable to life or growth
*“a hostile climate”*
*“an uncongenial atmosphere”*
6. unsolicited and resisted by the management of the target company ( used of attempts to buy or take control of a business)
*“hostile takeover”*
*“hostile tender offer”*
*n.* 1. troops belonging to the enemy's military forces
*“the platoon ran into a pack of hostiles”*

<a id="entry-h-002860"></a>
## hostile takeover

*n.* 1. **敵意收購 /敵意收購, 惡意收購 /惡意收購** — An attempted takeover of a company that is strongly resisted by the target company's management.
2. **惡意併購, 惡意收購** — a takeover that is resisted by the management of the target company

<a id="entry-h-002861"></a>
## hostile witness

*n.* 1. **敵對證人 /敵對證人** — A witness who intentionally gives unfavorable evidence against the party that called him or her as its witness.
2. **偏袒的目擊證人, 存敵意的證人, 敵對證人** — a witness whose relationship to the opposing party is such that his or her testimony may be prejudiced against the opposing party
*“a hostile witness can be asked leading questions and cross-examined”*

<a id="entry-h-002862"></a>
## hostility

**發音：** /hɒsˈtɪlɪti/
*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **敵意 /敵意** — The state of being hostile.
2. **仇恨, 反目, 敵對, 敵意, 敵視** — a state of deep-seated ill-will
3. **敵對行動** — violent action that is hostile and usually unprovoked
4. a hostile (very unfriendly) disposition
*“he could not conceal his hostility”*
5. the feeling of a hostile person
*“he could no longer contain his hostility”*

<a id="entry-h-002863"></a>

## hot

*adj.* 1. **氣溫高+的, 溫度高+的, 灼熱+的, 炎熱+的, 燙+的** — used of physical heat; having a high or higher than desirable temperature or giving off heat or feeling or causing a sensation of heat or burning
*“hot stove”*
*“hot water”*
2. **兇猛+的, 猛烈+的, 狂暴+的** — characterized by violent and forceful activity or movement; very intense
*“the fighting became hot and heavy”*
*“a hot engagement”*
3. **剛出版+的, 急躁+的, 才發行+的, 時新+的, 熱切+的** — extended meanings; especially of psychological heat; marked by intensity or vehemence especially of passion or enthusiasm
*“a hot temper”*
*“a hot topic”*
4. **濃重明亮+的, 暖色+的** — (color) bold and intense
*“hot pink”*
5. **性感+的, 火辣+的** — sexually excited or exciting
*“was hot for her”*
*“hot pants”*
6. **通緝+的** — wanted by the police
*“a hot suspect”*

<a id="entry-h-002983"></a>
## hover

**發音：** /ˈhɒvə/
*v.* 1. **彷徨, 搖擺不定, 徘徊, 猶豫, 搖擺** — be undecided about something; waver between conflicting positions or courses of action
*“He oscillates between accepting the new position and retirement”*
2. **盤旋, 逗留, 徘徊** — move to and fro
*“The shy student lingered in the corner”*
3. **懸停, 徘徊, 盤旋** — hang in the air; fly or be suspended above
4. **使漂浮, 懸浮, 翱翔, 飄浮空中, 使升空** — be suspended in the air, as if in defiance of gravity
*“The guru claimed that he could levitate”*
5. *(intransitive)* **翱翔, 高飛 /高飛, 懸停 /懸停, 㝼** — To remain stationary or float in the air.
6. *(figuratively, intransitive)* **彽徊, 盤旋 /盤旋, 縈繞 /縈繞** — Sometimes followed by over: to hang around or linger in a place, especially in an uncertain manner.

2. **氣墊船, 氣墊運載工具** — a craft capable of moving over water or land on a cushion of air created by jet engines

<a id="entry-h-002990"></a>

<a id="entry-h-002991"></a>
## how do you do

*phr.* 1. *(dated, formal)* **幸會 /幸會, 請多關照 /請多關照, 您好, 初次見面 /初次見面** — A greeting used upon being introduced to someone. Alternatives are how are you, pleased to meet you, and nice to meet you. (Sometimes used as a rhetorical question among familiar parties, depending on the region).

<a id="entry-h-002992"></a>
## how do you mean

<a id="entry-h-002993"></a>

<a id="entry-h-003013"></a>
## however

**發音：** /haʊˈɛvə/
*adv.* 1. *(informal, not-comparable)* **無論如何 /無論如何** — In any way that one likes or chooses; in a haphazard or spontaneous way.
2. despite anything to the contrary (usually following a concession)
*“although I'm a little afraid, however I'd like to try it”*
*“while we disliked each other, nevertheless we agreed”*
3. by contrast; on the other hand
*“the first part was easy; the second, however, took hours"”*
4. to whatever degree or extent
*“The results, however general, are important”*
*“they have begun, however reluctantly, to acknowledge the legitimacy of some of the opposition's concerns”*
5. in whatever way or manner
*“Victory, however it was brought about, was sweet”*
*“however he did it, it was very clever”*

<a id="entry-h-003014"></a>
## howitzer

**發音：** /ˈhaʊ.ɪts.ə(ɹ)/
*n.* 1. **榴彈炮 /榴彈炮** — A cannon that combines certain characteristics of field guns and mortars, delivering projectiles with medium velocities, usually with relatively high trajectories; normally a cannon with a tube length of 20 to 30 calibers.
2. **榴彈炮, 迫擊炮, 曲射炮** — a muzzle-loading high-angle gun with a short barrel that fires shells at high elevations for a short range

<a id="entry-h-003015"></a>
## howl

**發音：** /ˈhaʊ̯l/
*n.* 1. a long loud emotional utterance
*“he gave a howl of pain”*
*“howls of laughter”*
2. the long plaintive cry of a hound or a wolf
3. a loud sustained noise resembling the cry of a hound
*“the howl of the wind made him restless”*
*v.* 1. **吼** — To utter a loud, protracted, mournful sound or cry, as dogs and wolves often do.
2. emit long loud cries
*“wail in self-pity”*
*“howl with sorrow”*
3. cry loudly, as of animals
*“The coyotes were howling in the desert”*
4. make a loud noise, as of wind, water, or vehicles
*“The wind was howling in the trees”*
*“The water roared down the chute”*
5. laugh unrestrainedly and heartily

## Hubble constant

## hubble constant

## huffy

<a id="entry-h-003133"></a>
## hug

**發音：** /hʌɡ/
*v.* 1. **偎抱, 懷抱, 抱, 抱抱, 擁抱** — squeeze (someone) tightly in your arms, usually with fondness
*“Hug me, please”*
*“They embraced”*
2. *(reciprocal, transitive)* **擁抱 /擁抱, 摟抱 /摟抱, 偎抱, 摟摟 /摟摟, 抱抱** — To embrace by holding closely, especially in the arms.
3. *(intransitive)* **擁抱 /擁抱** — To cling closely together.
4. fit closely or tightly
*“The dress hugged her hips”*
*n.* 1. **擁抱 /擁抱, 摟抱 /摟抱, 偎抱** — A close embrace, especially when charged with an emotion such as affection, joy, relief, lust, anger, aggression, compassion, and the like, as opposed to being characterized by formality, equivocation or ambivalence (a half-embrace or "little hug").
2. **偎抱, 摟抱, 緊抱, 擁抱** — a tight or amorous embrace
*“come here and give me a big hug”*

## hum

**發音：** /ˈhʌm/
*v.* 1. *(intransitive)* **哼, 哼唱** — To make a sound from the vocal cords without pronouncing any real words, with one's lips closed.
2. **嗡嗡叫, 嗡嗡響** — be noisy with activity
*“This office is buzzing with activity”*
3. **發低哼聲, 哼聲, 輕敲** — sound with a monotonous hum
4. sing with closed lips
*“She hummed a melody”*
5. make a low continuous sound
*“The refrigerator is humming”*
*n.* 1. **忙, 忙碌, 繁忙** — the state of being or appearing to be actively engaged in an activity
*“they manifested all the busyness of a pack of beavers”*
*“there is a constant hum of military preparation”*
2. an Islamic fundamentalist group in Pakistan that fought the Soviet Union in Afghanistan in the 1980s; now operates as a terrorist organization primarily in Kashmir and seeks Kashmir's accession by Pakistan
3. a humming noise
*“the hum of distant traffic”*

## humane

**發音：** /hjuːˈmeɪn/
*adj.* 1. **人道+的, 人道主義+的, 人道主義者+的** — pertaining to or concerned with the humanities
*“humanistic studies”*
*“a humane education”*
2. **有人情, 人道的** — Having or showing concern for the pain or suffering of another; compassionate.
3. **高尚的, 高雅+的, 文雅+的** — showing evidence of moral and intellectual advancement
4. marked or motivated by concern with the alleviation of suffering

<a id="entry-h-003220"></a>
## humaneness

*n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **仁慈** — The property of being humane.
2. the quality of compassion or consideration for others (people or animals)

<a id="entry-h-003221"></a>
## humanism

**發音：** /ˈhjuːmənɪz(ə)m/
*n.* 1. *(capitalized, historical, often)* **人文主義 /人文主義** — Specifically, a cultural and intellectual movement in 14th-16th century Europe characterised by attention to classical culture and a promotion of vernacular texts, notably during the Renaissance.
2. *(uncountable, usually)* **人文主義 /人文主義** — An ethical system that centers on humans and their values, needs, interests, abilities, dignity and freedom; especially used for a secular one which rejects theistic religion and superstition.
3. *(uncountable, usually)* **人道主義 /人道主義** — Humanitarianism.
4. the doctrine that people's duty is to promote human welfare
5. the doctrine emphasizing a person's capacity for self-realization through reason; rejects religion and the supernatural
6. the cultural movement of the Renaissance; based on classical studies

<a id="entry-h-003222"></a>
## humanist

*n.* 1. a classical scholar or student of the liberal arts
2. an advocate of the principles of humanism; someone concerned with the interests and welfare of humans
*adj.* 1. **人文主義+的, 古典文化+的** — of or pertaining to Renaissance humanism
*“the humanistic revival of learning”*
2. **人道主義者+的, 人道主義+的** — of or pertaining to a philosophy asserting human dignity and man's capacity for fulfillment through reason and scientific method and often rejecting religion
*“the humanist belief in continuous emergent evolution"- Wendell Thomas”*
3. **人道+的, 人道主義+的, 人道主義者+的** — pertaining to or concerned with the humanities
*“humanistic studies”*
*“a humane education”*
4. marked by humanistic values and devotion to human welfare
*“a humane physician”*
*“released the prisoner for humanitarian reasons”*

<a id="entry-h-003223"></a>
## humanistic

*“the humanist belief in continuous emergent evolution"- Wendell Thomas”*

<a id="entry-h-003237"></a>
## humble

**發音：** /ˈhʌmbəl/
*adj.* 1. **低下+的, 卑下+的, 謙卑+的, 謙虛+的, 謙遜+的** — low or inferior in station or quality
*“a humble cottage”*
*“a lowly parish priest”*
2. **謙卑 /謙卑, 謙遜 /謙遜, 謙虛 /謙虛** — Having a low position or a low opinion of oneself; not proud, arrogant, or assuming; modest.
3. **不熟練+的** — used of unskilled work (especially domestic work)
4. marked by meekness or modesty; not arrogant or prideful
*“a humble apology”*
*“essentially humble...and self-effacing, he achieved the highest formal honors and distinctions"- B.K.Malinowski”*
5. of low birth or station (`base' is archaic in this sense)
*“baseborn wretches with dirty faces”*
*“of humble (or lowly) birth”*
*v.* 1. cause to be unpretentious
*“This experience will humble him”*
2. cause to feel shame; hurt the pride of
*“He humiliated his colleague by criticising him in front of the boss”*

*“not everyone regards humility as a virtue”*

<a id="entry-h-003263"></a>
## humor

*n.* 1. **幽默, 幽默感, 詼諧** — the trait of appreciating (and being able to express) the humorous
*“she didn't appreciate my humor”*
*“you can't survive in the army without a sense of humor”*
2. **心情, 情緒, 心境** — a characteristic (habitual or relatively temporary) state of feeling
*“whether he praised or cursed me depended on his temper at the time”*
*“he was in a bad humor”*
3. a message whose ingenuity or verbal skill or incongruity has the power to evoke laughter
4. the quality of being funny
*“I fail to see the humor in it”*
5. (Middle Ages) one of the four fluids in the body whose balance was believed to determine your emotional and physical state
*“the humors are blood and phlegm and yellow and black bile”*
6. the liquid parts of the body
*v.* 1. put into a good mood
