<a id="entry-h-003268"></a>
## humour

**發音：** /ˈhjuː.mə(ɹ)/
*n.* 1. *(UK, uncountable, usually)* **幽默, 諧謔 /諧謔** — The quality of being amusing, comical, funny.
2. **心情, 情緒, 心境** — a characteristic (habitual or relatively temporary) state of feeling
*“whether he praised or cursed me depended on his temper at the time”*
*“he was in a bad humor”*
3. *(UK, uncountable, usually)* **心情** — A mood, especially a bad mood; a temporary state of mind or disposition brought upon by an event; an abrupt illogical inclination or whim.
4. **幽默, 幽默感, 詼諧** — the trait of appreciating (and being able to express) the humorous
*“she didn't appreciate my humor”*
*“you can't survive in the army without a sense of humor”*
5. *(UK, archaic, historical)* **體液 /體液** — Any of the fluids in an animal body, especially the four "cardinal humours" of blood, yellow bile, black bile and phlegm that were believed to control the health and mood of the human body.
6. a message whose ingenuity or verbal skill or incongruity has the power to evoke laughter
*v.* 1. put into a good mood

<a id="entry-h-003269"></a>
## humourlessly

*adv.* 1. **一本正經+地, 缺乏幽默感+地, 非常嚴肅+地** — in a humorless manner
*“he reacted rather humorlessly to these rumors”*

## hunt

*“the detectives hounded the suspect until they found him”*

*“The dogs are running deer”*
6. oscillate about a desired speed, position, or state to an undesirable extent
*“The oscillator hunts about the correct frequency”*
*n.* 1. **狩獵 /狩獵, 打獵 /打獵** — The act of hunting.

## hybrid

**發音：** /ˈhaɪ.bɹɪd/
*n.* 1. **雜種 /雜種** — Offspring resulting from cross-breeding different entities, e.g. two different species or two purebred parent strains.
2. **雜交品種, 雜交種, 雜種** — (genetics) an organism that is the offspring of genetically dissimilar parents or stock; especially offspring produced by breeding plants or animals of different varieties or breeds or species
*“a mule is a cross between a horse and a donkey”*
3. **混合動力汽車 /混合動力汽車** — A hybrid vehicle, especially a car; a vehicle that runs on both fuel (gasoline or diesel) and electricity (battery or energy from the sun).
4. a word that is composed of parts from different languages (e.g., `monolingual' has a Greek prefix and a Latin root)
5. a composite of mixed origin
*“the vice-presidency is a hybrid of administrative and legislative offices”*
*adj.* 1. **混血** — Of a car, running on both fuel (gasoline or diesel) and electricity (battery or energy from the sun).
2. produced by crossbreeding

2. **疑心病, 疑病症** — chronic and abnormal anxiety about imaginary symptoms and ailments

## hypothesis

**發音：** /haɪˈpɒθɪsɪs/
*n.* 1. **假說, 理論, 論** — a tentative insight into the natural world; a concept that is not yet verified but that if true would explain certain facts or phenomena
*“a scientific hypothesis that survives experimental testing becomes a scientific theory”*
*“he proposed a fresh theory of alkalis that later was accepted in chemical practices”*
2. **假說 /假說, 假設 /假設, 假定** — A tentative conjecture explaining an observation, phenomenon or scientific problem and that can be tested by further observation, investigation, or experimentation.
3. *(general)* **假設 /假設, 假定, 假說 /假說, 前提** — An assumption taken to be true for the purpose of argument or investigation.
4. **假設 /假設** — The antecedent of a conditional statement.
5. **假設, 猜測, 推測** — a message expressing an opinion based on incomplete evidence
6. a proposal intended to explain certain facts or observations

<a id="entry-h-003863"></a>
## hypothesise

*v.* 1. **使理論化, 假設, 推測, 猜想, 猜測** — to believe especially on uncertain or tentative grounds
*“Scientists supposed that large dinosaurs lived in swamps”*

<a id="entry-h-003864"></a>

*v.* 1. **使理論化, 假設, 推測, 猜想, 猜測** — to believe especially on uncertain or tentative grounds
*“Scientists supposed that large dinosaurs lived in swamps”*
2. *(British, English, Oxford)* **假設 /假設, 推測 /推測** — To assume or assert tentatively on uncertain grounds.

## hypothetical

2. based primarily on surmise rather than adequate evidence
*“theories about the extinction of dinosaurs are still highly conjectural”*
*“the supposed reason for his absence”*
*n.* 1. a hypothetical possibility, circumstance, statement, proposal, situation, etc.
*“consider the following, just as a hypothetical”*

2. surgical removal of the uterus

<a id="entry-h-003892"></a>
## hysteresis

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **遲滯現象** — A property of a system such that an output value is not a strict function of the corresponding input, but also incorporates some lag, delay, or history dependence, and in particular when the response for a decrease in the input variable is different from the response for an increase. For example, a thermostat with a nominal setpoint of 75° might switch the controlled heat source on when the temperature drops below 74°, and off when it rises above 76°.
2. the lagging of an effect behind its cause; especially the phenomenon in which the magnetic induction of a ferromagnetic material lags behind the changing magnetic field

<a id="entry-h-003893"></a>
## hysteria

**發音：** /hɪˈstɪəɹiə/
*n.* 1. **發狂, 譫妄, 狂熱, 狂暴** — state of violent mental agitation
2. *(uncountable, usually)* **歇斯底里** — Behavior exhibiting excessive or uncontrollable emotions, in a wide range from joy to panic but usually including anxiety or fear.
3. **歇斯底里症, 癔病** — neurotic disorder characterized by violent emotional outbreaks and disturbances of sensory and motor functions
4. excessive or uncontrollable fear

<a id="entry-h-003894"></a>
## hysterical

**發音：** /hɪˈstɛɹɪkəl/
*adj.* 1. **歇斯底里的** — Of, or arising from hysteria.
2. **易發歇斯底里的** — Having, or prone to having hysterics.
3. **令人捧腹的** — Provoking uncontrollable laughter.
4. characterized by or arising from psychoneurotic hysteria
*“during hysterical conditions various functions of the human body are disordered"- Morris Fishbein”*
*“hysterical amnesia”*
5. marked by excessive or uncontrollable emotion
*“hysterical laughter”*
*“a mob of hysterical vigilantes”*

<a id="entry-h-003895"></a>
## hysterical neurosis

*n.* 1. **歇斯底里症, 癔病** — neurotic disorder characterized by violent emotional outbreaks and disturbances of sensory and motor functions

<a id="entry-h-003896"></a>
## hysterically

*adv.* 1. **歇斯底里+地** — in a hysterical manner
*“she screamed hysterically when she heard the news”*

<a id="entry-h-003897"></a>
## hysterics

*n.* 1. **歇斯底里爆發** — an attack of hysteria

*n.* 1. **豪豬科** — Old World porcupines

<a id="entry-h-003899"></a>
## hystricomorpha

*n.* 1. **豪豬型亞目** — an order of rodents including: porcupines; guinea pigs; chinchillas; etc.
